Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The detection operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 137. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 579 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to detection theory, we decompose the problem into 137 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 579. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
